# The semi-synthetic twin: scores (generated)

Generated by `python -m src.analysis.twin_report` (dataset **v4**); do not edit by hand. Design: `docs/analysis_plan.md` §14 item 19 and its amendment of 2026-11-16. **Synthetic heads only**: these numbers describe how the models behave under the twin's generators, not what is true in the aquifer. Each value: median over the worlds of a cell [range: min–max under 10 worlds, else 2.5–97.5 %]; inside a world, medians over the core wells scored in the drought phase. Arms: C distance rules, D knowledge graph (the export's tables), T the truth's own routing (oracle). Cell tag: e = ε, u, p = π, n = ν, timing, variant.

## Realised dials

u: share of the true pumping volume from pairs the graph does not attribute (core wells with a true attributed pair); ε: share of the graph's attributed pairs that are not true; π explained: share of the noise-free head's training variance that the pumping contribution has.

## Scores 3, 4 and seed disagreement

Score 3: share of wells with true pumping whose fitted pumping contribution correlates positively with the true one and has an sd within a factor 2. Score 4: recharge lag error, weeks: |a·n fitted − a·n true|, the means of the continuous Gamma laws (the model's kernels are weekly, truncated and normalised, so these are not the implemented mean delays; astra review point 8). Seed disagreement: RMSE between the two starts' pumping contributions / noise-free sd. Sign violations (RQ2): share of core wells and stresses whose fitted gain has the wrong sign (0 by construction under hard signs).

## Score 5: a −20 % cut of true-edge facilities in the drought

Median relative error of the fitted head change against the true one, and share of wells with its sign. **Per well** (the plan's definition, score of record; worlds of scores version 2): each well's own true facilities, one well at a time. **Plain-wide** (descriptive; the only version of the first worlds): every well's true facilities cut at once, so an arm's links to facilities true for other wells move its prediction too.

Test 2's flag (analysis plan §14 item 22, fixed before kernel version 13 was pulled): a world is flagged when its seed disagreement is above τ, the 95% quantile of the base grid's worlds of the same arm and setting (C, free: τ = 0.138 (240 grid worlds); C, pooled_l0.1_fixed3000: τ = 0.066 (240 grid worlds); D, free: τ = 0.152 (240 grid worlds); D, pooled_l0.1_fixed3000: τ = 0.045 (240 grid worlds)). Detection = flagged among the worlds with wrong reasons; false alarms = flagged among the others (nominally 5 % on the grid itself, by construction); under 5 worlds with wrong reasons the detection rate is not estimable.
